cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(black_scholes_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(black_scholes black_scholes.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(black_scholes PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(black_scholes m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield

    double t;           // Time to maturity

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

codex
The options are generated deterministically from their indices, so each rank can generate its own balanced slice without an input transfer. I’ll gather results in their original order only when `-r` needs them; validation will collect just the first ten prices. This keeps routine communication small and preserves the existing output hash.

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

-            printUsage(argv[0]);
-            return 1;
+

+    int settings[3] = {0, 0, 0}; // Run/help/error, validation, results.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {

-    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
-    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
-    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

+    }
+

+    int numRanks = 1;

+    try {

+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions,
                     const size_t firstOption = 0) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        const size_t index = firstOption + i;
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[index % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (index / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

struct OptionPartition {
    size_t first;
    size_t count;
};

// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
}

// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
    std::vector<double> results;
    std::vector<int> counts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;
    if (rank == 0) {
        results.resize(numResults);
        counts.resize(numRanks);
        displacements.resize(numRanks);
    }

    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
                                 : nullptr;

        if (rank == 0) {
            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
            }
        }

        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        first = end;
    }
    return results;
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, const int rank, const int numRanks) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Rank zero controls arguments and output; all ranks take the same path.
    int settings[3] = {0, 0, 0}; // Run/help/error, validation, results.
    if (rank == 0) {
        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
                validate = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
                printResults = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                settings[0] = 1;
                break;
            } else {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                settings[0] = 2;
                break;
            }
        }
        settings[1] = validate;
        settings[2] = printResults;
    }

    MPI_Bcast(settings, 3, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (settings[0] != 0) {
        return settings[0] == 1 ? 0 : 1;
    }
    unsigned long long optionCount = numOptions;
    MPI_Bcast(&optionCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    numOptions = static_cast<size_t>(optionCount);
    validate = settings[1] != 0;
    printResults = settings[2] != 0;

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }

    // Reproduce this rank's inputs locally, with no scatter or replicated data.
    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, local.count, local.first);
    std::vector<double> localResults(local.count);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Pricing options...\n");
    }
    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

    for (size_t i = 0; i < local.count; ++i) {
        localResults[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }

    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
    }

    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);
    std::vector<double> results;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        results = gatherResults(localResults, numOptions,
                                printResults ? numOptions : numChecks,
                                rank, numRanks);
    }

    if (rank == 0 && printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }

    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            std::vector<OptionInput> validationOptions;
            generateOptions(validationOptions, numChecks);
            printf("Validating results...\n");
            const bool valid = validateResults(validationOptions, results);
            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
            exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;
        }
        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
    return exitCode;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    if (MPI_Init(&argc, &argv) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        return 1;
    }

    int rank = 0;
    int numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);

    int exitCode = 1;
    try {
        exitCode = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, numRanks);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        // An allocation failure on one rank must not strand the other ranks.
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }

    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

codex
The MPI version builds successfully. It uses balanced contiguous slices, reports the slowest rank’s compute time, and gathers prices in their original order. I’m comparing results against the original across 1, 2, 3, 4, and 8 ranks, including empty inputs and cases with more ranks than options.

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;

+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);

+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);

+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);

-int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;
+}
+

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -c 'import json, os, re, statistics, subprocess
directory = \"/tmp/black-scholes-mpi-g9zbfzmy\"
serial = directory + '\\''/black_scholes_serial'\\''
binary = directory + '\\''/final-build/black_scholes'\\''
env = dict(os.environ, OMPI_ALLOW_RUN_AS_ROOT='\\''1'\\'', OMPI_ALLOW_RUN_AS_ROOT_CONFIRM='\\''1'\\'')
count = 4000003
def run(command):
    result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, timeout=60)
    assert result.returncode == 0, (command, result.stdout, result.stderr)
    duration = float(re.search(r'\\''Computation time: ([0-9.]+) ms'\\'', result.stdout).group(1))
    return result, duration
def normalized(output):
    return '\\''\\n'\\''.join(line for line in output.splitlines() if not line.startswith(('\\''Computation time:'\\'', '\\''Options per second:'\\'')))
serial_times = []
reference = None
for iteration in range(3):
    args = ['\\''-n'\\'', str(count)] + (['\\''-r'\\'', '\\''-v'\\''] if iteration == 0 else [])
    result, duration = run([serial] + args)
    if iteration == 0:
        reference = normalized(result.stdout)
    serial_times.append(duration)
print(json.dumps({'\\''serial_ms'\\'': serial_times, '\\''serial_median_ms'\\'': statistics.median(serial_times)}), flush=True)
for ranks in [1, 2, 4, 8]:
    times = []
    for iteration in range(3):
        args = ['\\''-n'\\'', str(count)] + (['\\''-r'\\'', '\\''-v'\\''] if iteration == 0 else [])
        result, duration = run(['\\''mpirun'\\'', '\\''--oversubscribe'\\'', '\\''--bind-to'\\'', '\\''core'\\'', '\\''--map-by'\\'', '\\''core'\\'', '\\''-np'\\'', str(ranks), binary] + args)
        if iteration == 0:
            assert normalized(result.stdout) == reference, (ranks, result.stdout, reference)
        times.append(duration)
    print(json.dumps({'\\''ranks'\\'': ranks, '\\''options'\\'': count, '\\''times_ms'\\'': times, '\\''median_ms'\\'': statistics.median(times), '\\''serial_speedup'\\'': statistics.median(serial_times) / statistics.median(times), '\\''exact_results'\\'': True}), flush=True)

+struct OptionPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.
+OptionPartition partitionOptions(const size_t numOptions, const int rank,
+                                 const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, remainder),
+            base + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+// Collect either all prices (-r) or only the validation prefix (-v).
+// Windows keep MPI counts and displacements valid even above INT_MAX options.
+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                                  const size_t numOptions, const size_t numResults,
+                                  const int rank, const int numRanks) {
+    std::vector<double> results;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        results.resize(numResults);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    const auto local = partitionOptions(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (size_t first = 0; first < numResults;) {
+        const size_t end = first + std::min(maxCount, numResults - first);
+        const size_t sendFirst = std::max(first, local.first);
+        const size_t sendEnd = std::min(end, local.first + local.count);
+        const int sendCount = sendEnd > sendFirst
+                            ? static_cast<int>(sendEnd - sendFirst) : 0;
+        const double* sendBuffer = sendCount > 0
+                                 ? localResults.data() + (sendFirst - local.first)
+                                 : nullptr;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < numRanks; ++peer) {
+                const auto part = partitionOptions(numOptions, peer, numRanks);
+                const size_t partFirst = std::max(first, part.first);
+                const size_t partEnd = std::min(end, part.first + part.count);
+                counts[peer] = partEnd > partFirst
+                             ? static_cast<int>(partEnd - partFirst) : 0;
+                displacements[peer] = counts[peer] > 0
+                                    ? static_cast<int>(partFirst - first) : 0;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? results.data() + first : nullptr,
+                    counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        first = end;
+    }
+    return results;

+// Contiguous, balanced ownership also preserves the original result order.

+    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t remainder = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);

+std::vector<double> gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
